framework-reference-city

framework-reference-test · city · PartialScope · 862 nodes · 0 connections

Engine-independent metadata. Images and artwork are optional consumers, not generation inputs.

Scope remains: Approved district and city composition, terrain and street geometry, exact map reconciliation, full terrain/object/motion asset inventory, approved production art and functional Godot world remain open.

Semantic layers

Dependency order is a declaration, not render order or proof of executed generation stages.

LayerPurpose/source ruleDepends onMembersScope

Without a declared layer: 862 nodes and 0 links. Layer membership does not certify physical coverage.
